```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {= {\left( {\left\lbrack {\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)} \right\rbrack \circ \left\lbrack {\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{A}})}} \right\rbrack} \right)\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}} \\
 & {= {\left\lbrack {\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{1},t,x_{2},\ldots,t,x_{d})}}} \\
 & {{= \left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{1})}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{2})}},\ldots,{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{d})}} \right)}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (II) proves that for all $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,0,0,\ldots,0)}} & {= \left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,0)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,0)}},\ldots,{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,0)}} \right)} \\
 & {{= {(0,0,\ldots,0)} = 0}.}
\end{array}
```

Next note that (II) and (320) imply that for all $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} & {= \left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x_{1})}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x_{2})}},\ldots,{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x_{d})}} \right)} \\
 & {{= {(0,0,\ldots,0)} = 0}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that the triangle inequality and the fact that for all $`r \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$, $`{(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

```math
\left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|x_{j}|}^{2\hspace{0pt}r}} \right\rbrack^{1/2} \leq \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|x_{j}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack^{r/2}
```

prove that for all $`b \in {\mathbb{R}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`r \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
\left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}\left( {{|b|} + {|x_{j}|}^{r}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2} & {\leq {\left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}b^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2} + \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|x_{j}|}^{2\hspace{0pt}r}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {{{|b|}\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|x_{j}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack^{r/2}} = {{{|b|}\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + \left\| x \right\|^{r}}}.}
\end{array}
```

This, (III), and (320) assure that for all $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {\left\| {{t\hspace{0pt}x} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}} \right\| = \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|{{t\hspace{0pt}x_{j}} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{j})}}}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}} \\
 & {\leq \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}\left\lbrack {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}{\max\left\{ 1,{|t|}^{q},{|x_{j}|}^{q} \right\}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2} \leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}\left\lbrack {{\max\left\{ 1,{|t|}^{q} \right\}} + {|x_{j}|}^{q}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {\max\left\{ 1,{|t|}^{q} \right\}} \right\rbrack} + \left\| x \right\|^{q}} \right)}}.}
\end{array}
```

Furthermore, observe that (IV), (320), and (324) show that for all $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
\left\| {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right\| & {= \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}{|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x_{j})}}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}} \\
 & {\leq \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}\left( {1 + {2\hspace{0pt}{|t|}^{2}} + {2\hspace{0pt}{|x_{j}|}^{2}}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}} \\
 & {= \left\lbrack {\sum_{j = 1}^{d}\left( {1 + {2\hspace{0pt}{|t|}^{2}} + {|{\sqrt{2}\hspace{0pt}x_{j}}|}^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{1/2}} \\
 & {{\leq {{\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {2\hspace{0pt}{|t|}^{2}}} \right)} + \left\| {\sqrt{2}\hspace{0pt}x} \right\|^{2}} = {{\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {2\hspace{0pt}{|t|}^{2}}} \right)} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\| x\|}^{2}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, note that Corollary 2.21 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") implies that

```math
{{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)} \leq {d^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}}}.
```

Item (ii calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) in Corollary 2.9 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), (V), and (317) hence ensure that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} & {\leq {\left\lbrack {\max\left\{ 1,\frac{{\mathcal{I}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{A}})}} + 1}{{\mathcal{I}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}})}} + 1} \right\}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {\left\lbrack {\max{\{ 1,\frac{d + 2}{{2\hspace{0pt}d} + 1}\}}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)} = {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)}} \\
 & {{\leq {d^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}} \leq {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack \frac{360\hspace{0pt}q}{({q - 2})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\log_{2}{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + q + 1} \right\rbrack} - {252\hspace{0pt}d^{2}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Next note that item (ii calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) in Proposition 2.6 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), (VI), and (317) demonstrate that

```math
\begin{aligned}
{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} & {{= {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi,\Psi,\ldots,\Psi)}} \right)} = {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi)}} \leq {\frac{q}{({q - 2})}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{\log_{2}{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + q}\rbrack}}}.}
\end{aligned}
```

This, the fact that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$, (321), (322), (325), (326), and (328) establish items (i)–(vi). The proof of Proposition 3.7 is thus completed. ∎

### 3.3 Space-time ANN approximations for Euler approximations

#### 3.3.1 Space-time representations for Euler approximations

###### Lemma 3.8.

Let $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\mu \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$, $`T \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(t_{n})}_{n \in {\{{- 1},0,1,\ldots,{N + 1}\}}} \subseteq {\mathbb{R}}`$ satisfy that $`t_{- 1} < 0 = t_{0} < t_{1} < \ldots < t_{N} = T < t_{N + 1}`$, let $`f_{n}:{{\mathbb{R}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, be the functions which satisfy for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that

```math
{{f_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} = {{\left\lbrack \frac{({t - t_{n - 1}})}{({t_{n} - t_{n - 1}})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(t_{n - 1},t_{n}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\left\lbrack \frac{({t_{n + 1} - t})}{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(t_{n},t_{n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}},
```

and let $`{Y = {(Y_{t}^{x,y})}_{{(t,x,y)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}}}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {Y_{t}^{x,y} = {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {\frac{({t - t_{n}})}{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}} \right\rbrack}}}
\end{array}
```

(cf. Definition 2.1). ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") and Definition 2.3. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). Then

1.  (i)
    it holds that

    ```math
    \begin{matrix}
    \left( {{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}} \ni {(t,x,y)}\mapsto Y_{t}^{x,y} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right) \\
    {\in {C\hspace{0pt}{({{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}}
    \end{matrix}
    ```

    and

2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{array}{r}
    {{Y_{t}^{x,y} = {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{f_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}\hspace{0pt}Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}}}}.}
    \end{array}
    ```

###### Proof of Lemma 3.8.

Observe that (331) ensures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t_{n + 1} - t}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)} + {Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 - \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}}} \right)} + {Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 - \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}}} \right)}} \\
 & {+ {\left( {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {\frac{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}} \right\rbrack}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)}} \\
 & {{= {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {\left\lbrack {{{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)}} = Y_{t}^{x,y}}.}
\end{array}
```

Hence, we obtain that for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {Y_{t}^{x,y} = {{Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{\{ t_{0}\}}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N - 1}\left( {Y_{t}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{\{ t_{0}\}}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N - 1}\left( {\left\lbrack {{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t_{n + 1} - t}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)} + {Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n}}{t_{n + 1} - t_{n}} \right)}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{\{ t_{0}\}}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + \left\lbrack {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N - 1}{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n + 1}}{t_{n} - t_{n + 1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {{+ \left\lbrack {\sum\limits_{n = 1}^{N}{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{n - 1}}{t_{n} - t_{n - 1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{n - 1},t_{n}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}} \right\rbrack}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (330) implies that for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
Y_{t}^{x,y} & {= {{Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{\{ t_{0}\}}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{1}}{t_{0} - t_{1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{0},t_{1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {Y_{t_{N}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t - t_{N - 1}}{t_{N} - t_{N - 1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{N - 1},t_{N}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}} \\
 & {+ {\sum\limits_{n = 1}^{N - 1}{Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\left( \frac{t - t_{n + 1}}{t_{n} - t_{n + 1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\left( \frac{t - t_{n - 1}}{t_{n} - t_{n - 1}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{(t_{n - 1},t_{n}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}} \right\rbrack}}} \\
 & {= {{Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}\left( \frac{t_{1} - t}{t_{1} - t_{0}} \right)\hspace{0pt}\, 1_{\lbrack t_{0},t_{1}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {Y_{t_{N}}^{x,y}\hspace{0pt}f_{N}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\sum\limits_{n = 1}^{N - 1}{f_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}\hspace{0pt}Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}}}}} \\
 & {{= {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{f_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}\hspace{0pt}Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Next we claim that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ it holds that

```math
{\left( {{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}} \ni {(x,y)}\mapsto Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right) \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}}.
```

We now prove (337) by induction on $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$. Note that the fact that for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that $`Y_{t_{0}}^{x,y} = Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ proves (337) in the base case $`n = 0`$. For the induction step assume there exists $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$ which satisfies that

```math
{\left( {{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}} \ni {(x,y)}\mapsto Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right) \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}}.
```

Observe that (331) ensures that for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y} = {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {{({t_{n + 1} - t_{n}})}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (338) and the hypothesis that $`\mu \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$ demonstrates that

```math
{\left( {{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}} \ni {(x,y)}\mapsto Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right) \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}}.
```

Induction thus proves (337). Next observe that (336), (337), and the fact that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ it holds that $`f_{n} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ show that

```math
\begin{matrix}
\left( {{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}} \ni {(t,x,y)}\mapsto Y_{t}^{x,y} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right) \\
{{\in {C\hspace{0pt}{({{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}}.}
\end{matrix}
```

Combining this with (336) establishes items (i)–(ii). The proof of Lemma 3.8 is thus completed. ∎

#### 3.3.2 ANN representations for hat functions

###### Lemma 3.9.

Let $`a \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ satisfy for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, let $`{\alpha,\beta,\gamma,h} \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ satisfy that $`\alpha < \beta < \gamma`$, let $`W_{1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{4 \times 1}`$, $`B_{1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{4}`$, $`W_{2} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{1 \times 4}`$, $`B_{2} \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ satisfy that

```math
{{W_{1} = \begin{pmatrix}
\frac{1}{({\beta - \alpha})} \\
\frac{1}{({\beta - \alpha})} \\
\frac{1}{({\gamma - \beta})} \\
\frac{1}{({\gamma - \beta})}
\end{pmatrix}},{B_{1} = \begin{pmatrix}
{- \frac{\alpha}{({\beta - \alpha})}} \\
{- \frac{\beta}{({\beta - \alpha})}} \\
{- \frac{\beta}{({\gamma - \beta})}} \\
{- \frac{\gamma}{({\gamma - \beta})}}
\end{pmatrix}}},
```

```math
{{W_{2} = \begin{pmatrix}
h & {- h} & {- h} & h
\end{pmatrix}},{B_{2} = 0}},
```

and let $`\Phi \in {{({{\mathbb{R}}^{4 \times 1} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{4}})} \times {({{\mathbb{R}}^{1 \times 4} \times {\mathbb{R}}})}} \subseteq \mathbf{N}`$ satisfy that $`\Phi = {({(W_{1},B_{1})},{(W_{2},B_{2})})}`$ (cf. Definition 2.1). ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). Then

1.  (i)
    it holds that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ and
2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{array}{cl}
    {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} & {= {{\left\lbrack \frac{{({t - \alpha})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\beta - \alpha})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\alpha,\beta\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\left\lbrack \frac{{({\gamma - t})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\gamma - \beta})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\beta,\gamma)}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}} \\
     & {= \begin{cases}
    0 & {:{t \in {{({- \infty},\alpha\rbrack} \cup {\lbrack\gamma,\infty)}}}} \\
    \frac{{({t - \alpha})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\beta - \alpha})} & {:{t \in {(\alpha,\beta\rbrack}}} \\
    \frac{{({\gamma - t})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\gamma - \beta})} & {:{t \in {(\beta,\gamma)}}}
    \end{cases}}
    \end{array}
    ```

(cf. Definition 2.3. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")).

###### Proof of Lemma 3.9.

Observe that for all $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} = {{W_{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\mathfrak{M}}_{a,4}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{1}\hspace{0pt}t} + B_{1}})}} \right)} + B_{2}}} \\
 & {= {{{h\hspace{0pt}{\max\left\{ \frac{({t - \alpha})}{({\beta - \alpha})},0 \right\}}} - {h\hspace{0pt}{\max\left\{ \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\beta - \alpha})},0 \right\}}} - {h\hspace{0pt}{\max\left\{ \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\gamma - \beta})},0 \right\}}}} + {h\hspace{0pt}{\max\left\{ \frac{({t - \gamma})}{({\gamma - \beta})},0 \right\}}}}} \\
 & {= {{h\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{0 - 0 - 0} + 0}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{({- \infty},\alpha\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {h\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{({t - \alpha})}{({\beta - \alpha})} - 0 - 0} + 0} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\alpha,\beta\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}} \\
 & {+ {h\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{({t - \alpha})}{({\beta - \alpha})} - \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\beta - \alpha})} - \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\gamma - \beta})}} + 0} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\beta,\gamma)}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}} \\
 & {+ {h\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{({t - \alpha})}{({\beta - \alpha})} - \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\beta - \alpha})} - \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\gamma - \beta})}} + \frac{({t - \gamma})}{({\gamma - \beta})}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{\lbrack\gamma,\infty)}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}} \\
 & {= {{h\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack \frac{({t - \alpha})}{({\beta - \alpha})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\alpha,\beta\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {h\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {1 - \frac{({t - \beta})}{({\gamma - \beta})}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\beta,\gamma)}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}} \\
 & {= {{\left\lbrack \frac{{({t - \alpha})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\beta - \alpha})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\alpha,\beta\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} + {\left\lbrack \frac{{({\gamma - t})}\hspace{0pt}h}{({\gamma - \beta})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\mathbb{1}_{(\beta,\gamma)}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}}}
\end{array}
```

(cf. Definition 2.2. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). The proof of Lemma 3.9 is thus completed. ∎

#### 3.3.3 A posteriori error estimates for space-time ANN approximations

###### Proposition 3.10.

Let $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`a \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ satisfy for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, let $`T \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(t_{n})}_{n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}} \subseteq {\mathbb{R}}`$ satisfy for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ that $`t_{n} = \frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}`$, let $`{\mathfrak{D}} \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`q \in {(2,\infty)}`$ satisfy that

```math
{{\mathfrak{D}} = {{\left\lbrack \frac{720\hspace{0pt}q}{({q - 2})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\log_{2}{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + q + 1} \right\rbrack} - 504}},
```

let $`\Phi \in \mathbf{N}`$ satisfy that $`{\mathcal{I}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} = {\mathcal{O}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} = d`$, and let $`{Y = {(Y_{t}^{x,y})}_{{(t,x,y)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}}}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {Y_{t}^{x,y} = {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {\left( {\frac{t\hspace{0pt}N}{T} - n} \right)\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{T}{N}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}} \right\rbrack}}}
\end{array}
```

(cf. Definition 2.1). ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") and Definition 2.3. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). Then there exist $`\Psi_{y} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$, such that

1.  (i)
    it holds for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$,
2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{q} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)}},
    ```

3.  (iii)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{2} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}} \right)}}},
    ```
