Corollary 2.24 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") (with $`a = a`$, $`n = 2`$, $`L = {\max{\{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}},{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}\}}}`$, $`{\mathfrak{i}}_{1} = 2`$, $`{\mathfrak{i}}_{2} = {2\hspace{0pt}d}`$, $`\Psi = {({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}`$, $`\Phi = {(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}`$ for $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ in the notation of Corollary 2.24 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")), (IV), and the fact that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}} = 13`$ hence prove that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left. ({P_{2,{({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}}{(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}} \right)} \leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}} + {6\hspace{0pt}{({{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}} - 3})}} + 3 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}} \right)^{2}}} \\
 & {= {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {11 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}} \right)^{2}}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {11 + {6\hspace{0pt}\left( {2 + {n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right)} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {n\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}}} \\
 & {{= {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {23 + {6\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {n\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Moreover, observe that (346) and (e) imply that $`{{2\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} \leq {d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}}}.`$ Combining this with Proposition 2.16 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), (364), and the fact that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} = {{4\hspace{0pt}d^{2}} + {3\hspace{0pt}d}} \leq {4\hspace{0pt}{({d^{2} + d})}}`$ ensures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{n,y})}} & {= {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\Gamma \odot_{{\mathbb{I}}_{d + 1}}{\lbrack{P_{2,{({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}}{(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}}\rbrack}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {{\max\left\{ 1,\frac{2\hspace{0pt}{({d + 1})}}{({d + 1})} \right\}}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({P_{2,{({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}}{(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}})}}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}} + \left( {23 + {6\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {n\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}} + \left( {23 + {6\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {4\hspace{0pt}d^{2}} + {3\hspace{0pt}d} + {n\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{2\hspace{0pt}{({d^{2} + d})}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Next note that (III), (363), (69. ‣ 2.2.4 Powers and extensions of ANNs ‣ 2.2 Compositions of ANNs ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")), (118. ‣ 2.3.2 Parallelizations of ANNs with different lengths ‣ 2.3 Parallelizations of ANNs ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")), (101. ‣ 2.3.1 Parallelizations of ANNs with the same length ‣ 2.3 Parallelizations of ANNs ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")), item (ii calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) in Proposition 2.16 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), and item (i calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) in Lemma 2.13 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") demonstrate that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{n,y})}} & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left. ({P_{2,{({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}}{(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{2}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathcal{E}_{{\max{\{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}},{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}\}}},{\mathbb{I}}_{1}}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}},{\mathcal{E}_{{\max{\{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}},{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}\}}},{\mathbb{I}}_{d}}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathbf{P}_{2}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathcal{E}_{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}},{\mathbb{I}}_{1}}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}},{\mathcal{E}_{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}},{\mathbb{I}}_{d}}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\mathcal{E}_{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}},{\mathbb{I}}_{d}}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\left( {({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}^{\bullet 0} \right) \bullet \Xi_{n,y}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{I}}_{d})}^{\bullet 0} \right)} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}\left( \Xi_{n,y} \right)}} - 1}} \\
 & {= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}}} = {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + {\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}} + 1}} \\
 & {{= {{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + 2 + {n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Therefore, we obtain that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{N,y})}} - {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{n,y})}} - 1} & {= {{({{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + 2 + {N\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}})} - {({{\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}} + 2 + {n\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}})} - 1}} \\
 & {{= {{{({N - n})}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} - 1}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, note that (366) proves that for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that $`L_{y} = {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{N,y})}} = {\mathcal{L}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{N,0})}} = L_{0}`$. The fact that $`{\sum_{n = 0}^{N - 1}{({N - n})}} = {\sum_{m = 1}^{N}m} = {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}{({N + 1})}}`$, (362), and (367) hence assure that for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}} \\
 & {\leq \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack \left\lbrack \sum_{n = 0}^{N - 1}\left( 2\mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{n,y})} \right. \right. \right.} \\
 & {\left. \left. \left. + \max\left\{ \left( \mathcal{L}{(\Psi_{N,y})} - \mathcal{L}{(\Psi_{n,y})} - 1 \right)2d{(2d + 1)} + d{(2d + 1)},0 \right\} \right) \right\rbrack + \mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{N,y})} \right\rbrack^{2}} \\
 & {= \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack \left\lbrack \sum_{n = 0}^{N - 1}\left( 2\mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{n,y})} + \max\left\{ {(N - n)}\mathcal{H}{(\Phi)}2d{(2d + 1)} - d{(2d + 1)},0 \right\} \right) \right\rbrack \right.} \\
 & {\left. + \mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{N,y})} \right\rbrack^{2}} \\
 & {\leq \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack {(2N + 1)}\mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{N,y})} \right.} \\
 & {\left. + \max\left\{ \mathcal{H}{(\Phi)}2d{(2d + 1)}\left\lbrack \sum_{n = 0}^{N - 1}{(N - n)} \right\rbrack - Nd{(2d + 1)},0 \right\} \right\rbrack^{2}} \\
 & {{= {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{{({{2\hspace{0pt}N} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{N,y})}} + {\max\left\{ {{\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}\hspace{0pt}d\hspace{0pt}{({{2\hspace{0pt}d} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}{({N + 1})}} - {N\hspace{0pt}d\hspace{0pt}{({{2\hspace{0pt}d} + 1})}}},0 \right\}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

This and (365) imply that for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{matrix}
{\mathcal{P}{(\Phi_{y})} \leq \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack {(2N + 1)}\left\lbrack d^{2}{\mathfrak{D}} + \left( 23 + 6N\mathcal{H}{(\Phi)} + 4d^{2} + 3d + N\left\lbrack 2{(d^{2} + d)} + \mathcal{P}{(\Phi)} \right\rbrack^{2} \right)^{2} \right\rbrack \right.} \\
{\left. + \max\left\{ \mathcal{H}{(\Phi)}d{(2d + 1)}N{(N + 1)} - Nd{(2d + 1)},0 \right\} \right\rbrack^{2}.}
\end{matrix}
```

Therefore, we obtain that for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}} \\
 & {\leq \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack {(2N + 1)}\left\lbrack d^{2}{\mathfrak{D}} + \left( 23 + 6N\mathcal{H}{(\Phi)} + 7d^{2} + N\left\lbrack 4d^{2} + \mathcal{P}{(\Phi)} \right\rbrack^{2} \right)^{2} \right\rbrack \right.} \\
 & {\left. + \max\left\{ \mathcal{H}{(\Phi)}d{(2d + 1)}N{(N + 1)} - Nd{(2d + 1)},0 \right\} \right\rbrack^{2}} \\
 & {{\leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{3\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}} + \left( {23 + {6\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {7\hspace{0pt}d^{2}} + {N\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{4\hspace{0pt}d^{2}} + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack} + {6\hspace{0pt}d^{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, note that (354), (II), and Lemma 3.8 (with $`N = N`$, $`d = d`$, $`\mu = {\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}`$, $`T = T`$, $`{({{\{{- 1},0,1,\ldots,{N + 1}\}} \ni n\mapsto t_{n} \in {\mathbb{R}}})} = {({{\{{- 1},0,1,\ldots,{N + 1}\}} \ni n\mapsto t_{n} \in {\mathbb{R}}})}`$, $`{({{\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}} \ni n\mapsto f_{n} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}})} = {({{\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}} \ni n\mapsto{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}})}`$, $`Y = Y`$ in the notation of Lemma 3.8) ensure that for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{t}^{x,y} = {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}}} = {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Moreover, observe that (360), (361), item (iv calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) in Proposition 2.16 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") (with $`\Psi = {\mathbb{I}}_{d + 1}`$, $`\Phi_{1} = \Gamma`$, $`\Phi_{2} = {P_{2,{({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}}{(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}}`$, $`{\mathfrak{i}} = {2\hspace{0pt}{({d + 1})}}`$ for $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ in the notation of Proposition 2.16 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")), and Corollary 2.23 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations") (with $`a = a`$, $`n = 2`$, $`{\mathbb{I}} = {({\mathbb{I}}_{1},{\mathbb{I}}_{d})}`$, $`\Phi = {(\Pi_{n},\Xi_{n,y})}`$ for $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ in the notation of Corollary 2.23 calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")) demonstrate that for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
{{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} = {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}}}.
```

Next note that (354) shows that for all $`k \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {{\mathbb{R}}\backslash{(t_{k - 1},t_{k + 1})}}`$ it holds that

```math
{{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} = 0}.
```

Combining this, (371), and (372) with (b) proves that for all $`k \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{k},t_{k + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
Y_{t}^{x,y} & {= {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}}}} \\
 & {= {{{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{k,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}} + {{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k + 1})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{{k + 1},y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}}}}
\end{array}
```

and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {= {\sum\limits_{n = 0}^{N}{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{k,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}} \\
 & {{+ {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k + 1})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{{k + 1},y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}}.}
\end{array}
```

The triangle inequality, (c), and (d) hence establish that for all $`k \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{k},t_{k + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \\
 & {\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}\left\| {{{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}\rbrack}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}} \right\|}} \\
 & {\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}{\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {\max\left\{ 1,{|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}|}^{q} \right\}} \right\rbrack} + {\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|}^{q}} \right)}}}
\end{array}
```

and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} & {\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}\left\| {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)} \right\|}} \\
 & {{\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}\left( {{\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {2\hspace{0pt}{|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}}|}^{2}}} \right)} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|}^{2}}} \right)}}.}
\end{array}
```

Next note that (354) ensures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ it holds that $`0 \leq {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{n})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}} \leq 1`$. Combining this with (376), (377), and (II) demonstrates that for all $`k \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{k},t_{k + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} & {\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}{\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {\sqrt{d} + {\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|}^{q}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {{\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {\sqrt{d} + {\| Y_{t_{k}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)} + {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {\sqrt{d} + {\| Y_{t_{k + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)}}} \\
 & {= {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {\| Y_{t_{k}}^{x,y}\|}^{q} + {\| Y_{t_{k + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)}}
\end{array}
```

and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} & {\leq {\sum\limits_{n = k}^{k + 1}\left( {{3\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{n,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|}^{2}}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {{3\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\| Y_{t_{k}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}} + {3\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\| Y_{t_{k + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}}}} \\
 & {{= {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| Y_{t_{k}}^{x,y}\|}^{2} + {\| Y_{t_{k + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}} \right)}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Furthermore, observe that (372), (V), and (a) ensure that for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

```math
{\left\lbrack {{({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N} \ni y\mapsto{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right\rbrack \in {C\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N},{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \right)}}.
```

In addition, observe that (b), (372) and (373) demonstrate that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,t_{n}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{aligned}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {{= {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n}{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{k})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{k,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right)}}}.}
\end{aligned}
```

This and (VI) show that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,t_{n}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})}`$, $`z = {(z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ with $`{{\forall k} \in {{\mathbb{N}} \cap {\lbrack 0,n\rbrack}}}:{y_{k} = z_{k}}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {= {\sum\limits_{m = 0}^{n}{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}{({{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{m})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{m,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}})}}}} \\
 & {= {\sum\limits_{m = 0}^{n}{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Gamma)}})}\hspace{0pt}{({{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Pi_{m})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t)}},{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Xi_{m,z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}})}}}} \\
 & {{= {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (A), (370), (378), (379), and (380) establishes items (i)–(vi). The proof of Proposition 3.10 is thus completed. ∎

#### 3.3.4 A priori estimates for Euler approximations

###### Lemma 3.11.

Let $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`{c,C} \in {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$, $`{A_{1},A_{2},\ldots,A_{N}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d \times d}`$, let $`❙❙ \cdot ❙❙:{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ be a norm on $`{\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, let $`\left| \middle| \middle| \cdot \middle| \middle| \middle| :{\mathbb{R}}^{d \times d}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)} \right.`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`A \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d \times d}`$ that $`\left| \middle| \middle| A \middle| \middle| \middle| = \sup_{\{ x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}:❙❙x❙❙ \leq 1\}}❙❙Ax❙❙ \right.`$, let $`\mu:{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ be a function which satisfies for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

```math
❙❙\mu{(x)}❙❙ \leq C + c❙❙x❙❙,
```

and let $`{Y_{n} = {(Y_{n}^{x,y})}_{{(x,y)} \in {{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}}}:{{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, be the functions which satisfy for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{n + 1}^{x,y} = {Y_{n}^{x,y} + {A_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{n}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Then

1.  (i)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{array}{cl}
     & {Y_{n}^{x,y} = {x + {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n - 1}\left\lbrack {{A_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{k + 1}} \right\rbrack}}}
    \end{array}
    ```

    and

2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{array}{cl}
     & {❙❙Y_{n}^{x,y}❙❙} \\
     & {\leq \left( ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + \max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ \right)\exp{(c\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \rbrack \right.)}.}
    \end{array}
    ```

###### Proof of Lemma 3.11.

We claim that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{n}^{x,y} = {x + {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n - 1}\left\lbrack {{A_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{k + 1}} \right\rbrack}}}.}
\end{array}
```

We now prove (387) by induction on $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$. Observe that the hypothesis that for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ proves (387) in the base case $`n = 0`$. For the induction step note that (384) implies that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ with

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {Y_{n}^{x,y} = {x + {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n - 1}\left\lbrack {{A_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{k + 1}} \right\rbrack}}}
\end{array}
```

it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
Y_{n + 1}^{x,y} & {= {Y_{n}^{x,y} + {A_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{n}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}}} \\
 & {= {x + \left\lbrack {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n - 1}\left( {{A_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{k + 1}} \right)} \right\rbrack + \left( {{A_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{n}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}} \right)}} \\
 & {{= {x + \left\lbrack {\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n}\left( {{A_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}\mu\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{k + 1}} \right)} \right\rbrack}}.}
\end{array}
```

Induction thus proves (387). Observe that (387) establishes item (i). In addition, note that (387), the triangle inequality, and the fact that for all $`A \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d \times d}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that $`\left. ❙❙Ax❙❙ \leq \middle| \middle| \middle| A \middle| \middle| \middle| ❙❙x❙❙ \right.`$ demonstrate that for all $`m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {❙❙Y_{m}^{x,y}❙❙ \leq ❙❙x❙❙ + \left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 0}^{m - 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k + 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| ❙❙\mu\left( Y_{k}^{x,y} \right)❙❙ \right\rbrack + ❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 0}^{m - 1}y_{k + 1}❙❙.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (383) ensures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {❙❙Y_{m}^{x,y}❙❙ \leq ❙❙x❙❙ + \left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 0}^{m - 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k + 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| \left( C + c❙❙Y_{k}^{x,y}❙❙ \right) \right\rbrack + ❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙} \\
 & {= ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + ❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ + c\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 0}^{m - 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k + 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| ❙❙Y_{k}^{x,y}❙❙ \right\rbrack} \\
 & {\leq ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + \left\lbrack \max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ \right\rbrack + c\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 0}^{m - 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k + 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| ❙❙Y_{k}^{x,y}❙❙\rbrack \right..}
\end{array}
```

The time-discrete Gronwall inequality (cf., e.g., Hutzenthaler et al. ​\[23, Lemma 2.1\] (with $`N = n`$, $`\alpha = \left( ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + \max_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}❙❙\sum_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ \right)`$, $`\left. \beta_{0} = c \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{1} \middle| \middle| \middle| ,\beta_{1} = c \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{2} \middle| \middle| \middle| ,\ldots,\beta_{n - 1} = c \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| ,\epsilon_{0} = ❙❙Y_{0}^{x,y}❙❙,\epsilon_{1} = ❙❙Y_{1}^{x,y}❙❙,\ldots,\epsilon_{n} = ❙❙Y_{n}^{x,y}❙❙ \right.`$ for $`n \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,N\}}`$ in the notation of Hutzenthaler et al. \[23, Lemma 2.1\])) hence implies that for all $`n \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{aligned}
{❙❙Y_{n}^{x,y}❙❙} & {\leq \left( ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + \max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ \right)\exp{(c\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 0}^{n - 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k + 1} \middle| \middle| \middle| \rbrack \right.)}.}
\end{aligned}
```

The hypothesis that for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ therefore assures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{aligned}
{❙❙Y_{n}^{x,y}❙❙} & {\leq \left( ❙❙x❙❙ + C\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \right\rbrack + \max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}❙❙\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}❙❙ \right)\exp{(c\left\lbrack \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{n} \middle| \middle| \middle| A_{k} \middle| \middle| \middle| \rbrack \right.)}.}
\end{aligned}
```

This establishes item (ii). The proof of Lemma 3.11 is thus completed. ∎

#### 3.3.5 A priori error estimates for space-time ANN approximations

###### Theorem 3.12.

Let $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`{\mathfrak{C}} \in {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$, $`a \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ satisfy for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, let $`T \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(t_{n})}_{n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}} \subseteq {\mathbb{R}}`$ satisfy for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ that $`t_{n} = \frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}`$, let $`{\mathfrak{D}} \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`q \in {(2,\infty)}`$ satisfy that

```math
{{\mathfrak{D}} = {{\left\lbrack \frac{720\hspace{0pt}q}{({q - 2})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\log_{2}{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + q + 1} \right\rbrack} - 504}},
```

let $`\Phi \in \mathbf{N}`$ satisfy for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{\mathcal{I}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} = {\mathcal{O}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} = d`$ and $`{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {\| x\|}} \right)}`$, let $`{Y = {(Y_{t}^{x,y})}_{{(t,x,y)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}}}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`Y_{0}^{x,y} = x`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{t}^{x,y} = {Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} + {\left( {\frac{t\hspace{0pt}N}{T} - n} \right)\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{T}{N}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}\left( Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y} \right)} + y_{n + 1}}\rbrack \right.}}},}
\end{array}
```

and let $`g_{n}:{{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, be the functions which satisfy for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

```math
{g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}} = {\left( {{\| x\|} + {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}t_{n}} + {\max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}\left\| {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}} \right\|}} \right)\hspace{0pt}{\exp{({{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}t_{n}})}}}
```

(cf. Definition 2.1). ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), Definition 2.3. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), and Definition 3.6. ‣ 3.2.2 ANN approximations for multi-dimensional products ‣ 3.2 ANN approximations for products ‣ 3 ANN approximation results ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). Then there exist $`\Psi_{y} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$, such that

1.  (i)
    it holds for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$,
2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {({g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{q} + {({g_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{q}} \right)}},
    ```

3.  (iii)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2} + {({g_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2}} \right)}}},
    ```

4.  (iv)
    it holds for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{aligned}
    {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} & {{\leq {\frac{9}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{2\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {\mathfrak{D}} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + \left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{2}}},}
    \end{aligned}
    ```

5.  (v)
    it holds for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

    ```math
    {\left\lbrack {{({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N} \ni y\mapsto{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right\rbrack \in {C\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N},{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \right)}},
    ```

    and

6.  (vi)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,t_{n}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})}`$, $`z = {(z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ with $`{{\forall k} \in {{\mathbb{N}} \cap {\lbrack 0,n\rbrack}}}:{y_{k} = z_{k}}`$ that

    ```math
    {{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} = {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}.
    ```

###### Proof of Theorem 3.12.

Throughout this proof let $`\Psi_{y} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$, satisfy that

1.  (I)
    it holds for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$,
2.  (II)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{q} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)}},
    ```

3.  (III)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{2} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}} \right)}}},
    ```

4.  (IV)
    it holds for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{matrix}
    {\mathcal{P}{(\Psi_{y})} \leq \frac{1}{2}\left\lbrack 6d^{2}N^{2}\mathcal{H}{(\Phi)} \right.} \\
    {\left. + 3N\left\lbrack d^{2}{\mathfrak{D}} + \left( 23 + 6N\mathcal{H}{(\Phi)} + 7d^{2} + N\left\lbrack 4d^{2} + \mathcal{P}{(\Phi)} \right\rbrack^{2} \right)^{2} \right\rbrack \right\rbrack^{2},}
    \end{matrix}
    ```

5.  (V)
    it holds for all $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

    ```math
    {\left\lbrack {{({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N} \ni y\mapsto{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right\rbrack \in {C\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N},{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \right)}},
    ```

    and

6.  (VI)
    it holds for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,t_{n}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`{y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})}},{z = {(z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}`$ with $`{{\forall k} \in {{\mathbb{N}} \cap {\lbrack 0,n\rbrack}}}:{y_{k} = z_{k}}`$ that

    ```math
    {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} = {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}
    ```

(cf. Proposition 3.10). Note that (IV) ensures for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{6\hspace{0pt}d^{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {3\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}} + \left( {23 + {6\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {7\hspace{0pt}d^{2}} + {N\hspace{0pt}d^{4}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{6\hspace{0pt}d^{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {3\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{d^{2}\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{D}}} + {N^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{8}\hspace{0pt}\left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + \left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right)^{2}}} \right\rbrack}} \right\rbrack^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Hence, we obtain that for all $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}} & {\leq {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{6\hspace{0pt}d^{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{2}\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {3\hspace{0pt}N^{3}\hspace{0pt}d^{8}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\mathfrak{D}} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + \left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {\frac{9}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{2\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + {\mathfrak{D}} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}} + \left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi)}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{2}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that Lemma 3.11 and the hypothesis that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$ it holds that $`t_{n} = \frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}`$ demonstrate that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|} & {\leq {\left\lbrack {{\| x\|} + \frac{{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}T}{N} + {\max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}\left\| {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}} \right\|}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}{\exp\left( \frac{{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}T}{N} \right)}}} \\
 & {{= {\left\lbrack {{\| x\|} + {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}t_{n}} + {\max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}\left\| {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}} \right\|}} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}{\exp{({{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}t_{n}})}}} = {g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (II) and (III) ensures that for all $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack t_{n},t_{n + 1}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\|{Y_{t}^{x,y} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} & {\leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{q} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{q}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {({g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{q} + {({g_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{q}} \right)}}
\end{array}
```

and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} & {\leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| Y_{t_{n}}^{x,y}\|}^{2} + {\| Y_{t_{n + 1}}^{x,y}\|}^{2}} \right)}}} \\
 & {{\leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({g_{n}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2} + {({g_{n + 1}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2}} \right)}}}.}
\end{array}
```

This, (I), (V), (VI), and (408) establish items (i)–(vi). The proof of Theorem 3.12 is thus completed. ∎

###### Corollary 3.13.

Let $`{{\mathfrak{C}},T,{\mathfrak{d}}} \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`a \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ satisfy for all $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, let $`\Phi_{d} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{\mathcal{I}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} = {\mathcal{O}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} = d`$, $`{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\|} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {\| x\|}})}}`$, and $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}}`$, let $`{Y^{d,N} = {(Y_{t,x,y}^{d,N})}_{{(t,x,y)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}}}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$, $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, be the functions which satisfy for all $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in \left\lbrack \frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},\frac{{({n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}T}{N} \right\rbrack`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`Y_{0,x,y}^{d,N} = x`$ and

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{Y_{t,x,y}^{d,N} = {Y_{\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},x,y}^{d,N} + {\left( {\frac{t\hspace{0pt}N}{T} - n} \right)\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\frac{T}{N}\hspace{0pt}{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(Y_{\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},x,y}^{d,N})}} + y_{n + 1}}\rbrack \right.}}}.}
\end{array}
```

(cf. Definition 2.1). ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), Definition 2.3. ‣ 2.1 Artificial neural networks (ANNs) and their realizations ‣ 2 Artificial neural network (ANN) calculus ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations"), and Definition 3.6. ‣ 3.2.2 ANN approximations for multi-dimensional products ‣ 3.2 ANN approximations for products ‣ 3 ANN approximation results ‣ Space-time error estimates for deep neural network approximations for differential equations")). Then there exist $`C \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ and $`\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$, $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, such that

1.  (i)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$,
2.  (ii)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{Y_{t,x,y}^{d,N} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {C\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}N^{3/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {\| x\|}^{3} + {\| y\|}^{3}})}}},
    ```

3.  (iii)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {C\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {\| x\|}^{2} + {\| y\|}^{2}})}}},
    ```

4.  (iv)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}} \leq {C\hspace{0pt}d^{16 + {8\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack}},
    ```

5.  (v)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

    ```math
    {\left\lbrack {{({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N} \ni y\mapsto{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right\rbrack \in {C\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N},{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \right)}},
    ```

    and

6.  (vi)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})}`$, $`z = {(z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ with $`{{\forall k} \in {{\mathbb{N}} \cap {\lbrack 0,n\rbrack}}}:{y_{k} = z_{k}}`$ that

    ```math
    {{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} = {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}.
    ```

###### Proof of Corollary 3.13.

Throughout this proof let $`{\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,q} \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$, $`q \in {(2,\infty)}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, satisfy for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`q \in {(2,\infty)}`$ that

```math
{{\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,q} = {{\left\lbrack \frac{720\hspace{0pt}q}{({q - 2})} \right\rbrack\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{\log_{2}{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + q + 1} \right\rbrack} - 504}},
```

let $`c = {\max{\{{\exp{({{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}T})}},{\mathfrak{D}}_{1,3},{62 + {6\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}{({{\mathfrak{C}} + 1})}}}\}}}`$, and let $`g_{n}^{d,N}:{{{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \times {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, be the functions which satisfy for all $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

```math
{{g_{n}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}} = {\left( {{\| x\|} + \frac{{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}T}{N} + {\max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,n\}}}\left\| {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}} \right\|}} \right)\hspace{0pt}{\exp{(\frac{{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}n\hspace{0pt}T}{N})}}}}.
```

Note that Theorem 3.12 (with $`N = N`$, $`d = d`$, $`{\mathfrak{C}} = {\mathfrak{C}}`$, $`a = a`$, $`T = T`$, $`t_{n} = \frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}`$, $`{\mathfrak{D}} = {\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,3}`$, $`\varepsilon = \varepsilon`$, $`q = 3`$, $`\Phi = \Phi_{d}`$, $`Y = Y^{d,N}`$, $`g_{n} = g_{n}^{d,N}`$ for $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ in the notation of Theorem 3.12) implies that there exist $`\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y} \in \mathbf{N}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$, $`{N,d} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, which satisfy that

1.  (I)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that $`{\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d + 1},{\mathbb{R}}^{d})}}`$,
2.  (II)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},\frac{{({n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{Y_{t,x,y}^{d,N} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {{2\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {({g_{n}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{3} + {({g_{n + 1}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{3}} \right)}},
    ```

3.  (III)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},\frac{{({n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left( {{({g_{n}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2} + {({g_{n + 1}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2}} \right)}}},
    ```

4.  (IV)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ that

    ```math
    \begin{array}{cl}
     & {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}} \\
     & {{\leq {\frac{9}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{2\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} + {\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,3} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}\mathcal{H}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} + \left\lbrack {4 + {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}}} \right\rbrack^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{2}}},}
    \end{array}
    ```

5.  (V)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,T\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

    ```math
    {\left\lbrack {{({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N} \ni y\mapsto{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}} \right\rbrack \in {C\hspace{0pt}\left( {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N},{\mathbb{R}}^{d} \right)}},
    ```

    and

6.  (VI)
    it holds for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack 0,\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})}`$, $`z = {(z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ with $`{{\forall k} \in {{\mathbb{N}} \cap {\lbrack 0,n\rbrack}}}:{y_{k} = z_{k}}`$ it holds that

    ```math
    {{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} = {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,z})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}.
    ```

Observe that Jensen’s inequality implies that for all $`n \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`p \in {\lbrack 1,\infty)}`$, $`{(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{n}`$ it holds that

```math
{{|{x_{1} + \cdots + x_{n}}|}^{p} \leq {n^{p - 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{|x_{1}|}^{p} + \cdots + {|x_{n}|}^{p}})}}}.
```

Moreover, note that Hölder’s inequality shows that for all $`N \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
{{\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{N}{\| y_{k}\|}} = {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{N}{({1\hspace{0pt}{\| y_{k}\|}})}} \leq {N^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{N}{\| y_{k}\|}^{2}} \right)^{1/2}} = {N^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}{\| y\|}}}.
```

Combining (425), (II), and (419) therefore ensures that for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},\frac{{({n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\|{Y_{t,x,y}^{d,N} - {{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}}\|} \leq {2\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {({g_{N}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{3}})}}} \\
 & {= {2\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {\left( {{\| x\|} + {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}T} + {\max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}}\left\| {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k}} \right\|}} \right)^{3}\hspace{0pt}{\exp{({3\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}T})}}}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {2\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {9\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| x\|}^{3} + c^{3} + \left( {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{N}{\| y_{k}\|}} \right)^{3}} \right)\hspace{0pt}c^{3}}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {2\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {9\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| x\|}^{3} + c^{3} + {N^{3/2}\hspace{0pt}{\| y\|}^{3}}} \right)\hspace{0pt}c^{3}}} \right)}} \\
 & {\leq {2\hspace{0pt}c^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}N^{3/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {9\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| x\|}^{3} + 1 + {\| y\|}^{3}} \right)}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {2\hspace{0pt}c^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}N^{3/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}\left( {10 + {9\hspace{0pt}{\| x\|}^{3}} + {9\hspace{0pt}{\| y\|}^{3}}} \right)}} \\
 & {{\leq {20\hspace{0pt}c^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{1/2}\hspace{0pt}N^{3/2}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {\| x\|}^{3} + {\| y\|}^{3}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Next note that (III), (419), (425), and (426) imply that for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`n \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,{N - 1}\}}`$, $`t \in {\lbrack\frac{n\hspace{0pt}T}{N},\frac{{({n + 1})}\hspace{0pt}T}{N}\rbrack}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`y = {(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{N})} \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\|{{({\mathcal{R}_{a}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}\|} \leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {4\hspace{0pt}{({g_{N}^{d,N}\hspace{0pt}{(x,y)}})}^{2}}}} \\
 & {= 6\sqrt{d} + 4\left( \parallel x \parallel + {\mathfrak{C}}T + \max\limits_{m \in {\{ 0,1,\ldots,N\}}}\left( \parallel \sum\limits_{k = 1}^{m}y_{k} \parallel \right)^{2}\exp{(2{\mathfrak{C}}T)} \right.} \\
 & {\leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {12\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| x\|}^{2} + c^{2} + \left( {\sum\limits_{k = 1}^{N}{\| y_{k}\|}} \right)^{2}} \right)\hspace{0pt}c^{2}}}} \\
 & {\leq {{6\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}} + {12\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\| x\|}^{2} + c^{2} + {N\hspace{0pt}{\| y\|}^{2}}} \right)\hspace{0pt}c^{2}}}} \\
 & {{\leq {18\hspace{0pt}c^{4}\hspace{0pt}\sqrt{d}\hspace{0pt}N\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {\| x\|}^{2} + {\| y\|}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Furthermore, observe that (418) shows that for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
{({\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,3})}^{2} & {= \left( {{\frac{2160}{\ln{(2)}}\hspace{0pt}{\ln{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}}} + {\mathfrak{D}}_{1,3}} \right)^{2} \leq {{({\mathfrak{D}}_{1,3})}^{2}\hspace{0pt}{({{\ln{(\varepsilon^{- 1})}} + 1})}^{2}}} \\
 & {{\leq {c^{2}\hspace{0pt}{|{1 - {\ln{(\varepsilon)}}}|}^{2}} \leq {2\hspace{0pt}c^{2}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

This, (IV), the hypothesis that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Phi_{d})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}}`$, and (425) assure that for all $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$, $`{d,N} \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`y \in {({\mathbb{R}}^{d})}^{N}`$ it holds that

```math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\Psi_{\varepsilon,d,N,y})}} \leq {\frac{9}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}} + {\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,3} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}} + {\lbrack{4 + {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}}}\rbrack}^{2}} \right)^{2}} \right\rbrack^{2}}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{27}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{4\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} + {({\mathfrak{D}}_{\varepsilon,3})}^{2} + \left( {30 + {6\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}} + {2\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {16 + {{\mathfrak{C}}^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}}} \right\rbrack}} \right)^{4}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{27}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{4\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} + {2\hspace{0pt}c^{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right)} + \left( {62 + {6\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{\mathfrak{d}}} + {2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}}} \right)^{4}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{27}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{4\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}^{2}\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} + {2\hspace{0pt}c^{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right)} + \left( {62 + {6\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\mathfrak{C}} + 1} \right)\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}}} \right)^{4}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {\leq {\frac{27}{2}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{c\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} + {2\hspace{0pt}c^{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right)} + \left( {c\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} \right)^{4}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {\leq {27\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {{c^{2}\hspace{0pt}\left( {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right)} + \left( {c\hspace{0pt}d^{2\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}} \right)^{4}} \right\rbrack}} \\
 & {{\leq {54\hspace{0pt}c^{4}\hspace{0pt}N^{6}\hspace{0pt}d^{16 + {8\hspace{0pt}{\mathfrak{d}}}}\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {1 + {|{\ln{(\varepsilon)}}|}^{2}} \right\rbrack}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining (I), (427), (428), (430), (V), and (VI) establishes items (i)-(vi). The proof of Corollary 3.13 is thus completed. ∎
